# Issue specs

One file per canonical issue of Linear team PAP: every non-archived issue from PAP-13 upward whose state is not Duplicate (PAP-1..PAP-12 are Justin's brief, archived strays and duplicates and are skipped). Frontmatter carries the Linear identifier, project, phase, type, priority, surfaces, milestone, state, relations, key, URL, `updatedAt`, the `model` and `effort` for the builder session (read from the issue's `Model` and `Effort` labels, the same values as `plan/model-effort.json`; umbrellas carry `model: null`, see `docs/cost-and-duration-estimate.md` section 4b), and since round 4 the Linear `estimate` (Fibonacci points), `dueDate` and `cycle`; the body is the live Linear description.

Linear is the system of record. Every file here is regenerated from `plan/linear-snapshot-live.json`, taken 2026-09-18T14:58:27Z after round 4 (2026-09-18) created the gap and sub-feature issues from the per-project feature matrices, the five new projects (Tenant AI Assistant, Workflows & Documents, Scheduling & Engagement, Commerce & Operations, Platform Operations) and turned on estimates, due dates, cycles and triage; see `plan/round4/` and `docs/linear-features.md`. File name: `<identifier>-<short title slug>.md` under the project key.

Files: 983. Ready for Claude at snapshot time: 30 (PAP-13, PAP-14, PAP-25, PAP-31, PAP-44, PAP-46, PAP-55, PAP-56, PAP-66, PAP-79, PAP-91, PAP-92, PAP-103, PAP-114, PAP-127, PAP-139, PAP-150, PAP-161, PAP-162, PAP-176, PAP-188, PAP-198, PAP-209, PAP-210, PAP-219, PAP-279, PAP-302, PAP-433, PAP-555, PAP-754). Backlog: 871. Triage: 82. Umbrellas (issues with sub-issues): 86. Deferred label: 204.
